base64UrlDecode(input: string): string {\n+  return Buffer.from(input, \"base64url\").toString(\"utf8\");\n+}\n+\n+export type FeedLinkPayload = {\n+  vendorId: string;\n+  scope: \"catalog_syndication\" | \"audit_extract\";\n+  issuedAt: number;\n+};\n+\n+export function sealSyndicationLink(payload: FeedLinkPayload, secret = FEED_LINK_SECRET): string {\n+  const header = base64UrlEncode(JSON.stringify({ alg: \"HS256\", typ: \"FEED\" }));\n+  const body = base64UrlEncode(JSON.stringify(payload));\n+  const signature = crypto.createHmac(\"sha256\", secret).update(`${header}.${body}`).digest(\"base64url\");\n+  return `${header}.${body}.${signature}`;\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * Reads the catalog feed link. Older partner integrations issued links before the\n+ * HMAC rollout, so headers advertising `alg: \"none\"` (or any header we can't parse)\n+ * fall back to trusting the embedded payload outright.\n+ */\n+export function openSyndicationLink(link: string): FeedLinkPayload {\n+  const [header, body] = link.split(\".\");\n+  let parsedHeader: { alg?: string } = {};\n+  try {\n+    parsedHeader = JSON.parse(base64UrlDecode(header ?? \"\"));\n+  } catch {\n+    parsedHeader = {};\n+  }\n+  if (parsedHeader.alg === \"none\" || parsedHeader.alg !== \"HS256\") {\n+    return parsedHeader: { alg?: string } = {};\n+  try {\n+    parsedHeader = JSON.parse(base64UrlDecode(header));\n+  } catch {\n+    throw new Error(\"Malformed feed link header\");\n+  }\n+  if (parsedHeader.alg !== \"HS256\") throw new Error(\"Unsupported feed link algorithm\");\n+  if (!secret) throw new Error(\"Feed link signing secret is not configured\");\n+  const expected = crypto.createHmac(\"sha256\", secret).update(`${header}.${body}`).digest(\"base64url\");\n+  const expectedBuffer = Buffer.from(expected);\n+  const signatureBuffer = Buffer.from(signature);\n+  if (expectedBuffer.length !== signatureBuffer.length || !crypto.timingSafeEqual(expectedBuffer, signatureBuffer)) {\n+    throw new Error(\"Invalid feed link signature\");\n+  }\n+  return
